# Canon projection editorial review

Generated from the actual merged runtime registry. Rebuild with `node scripts/evaluation/buildCanonProjectionReview.js` after registry changes. Runtime availability is an implementation status, never human approval.

Baseline full projections: 435 (145 passages × three decks). Matched-pair projections: 90 (30 independently authored general pairs × three decks). Active enrichment projections: 0. Total rows: 525.

Every human review below is pending. Review meaning fidelity, assumptions about unprovided life events, absent-stage implications, selected-deck names, and card/deck support for each visual anchor. A scene-word blacklist cannot establish semantic safety. Context-specific pair prose is not inherited from complete triads.

Enrichment review keeps full prose approval (`human_verdict`) separate from projection approval (`projection_verdict`). New records carry a stable `source_id`, source URL/method/title, `projection_text`, and `projection_version`. A blank or rejected projection verdict omits the meaning from cross-deck generation with `missing_or_unapproved_meaning_projection`; approval of an old full passage never grants projection approval. Regenerating a queue preserves reviewed rows and their existing columns exactly when no new drafts are added. General-context enrichment remains excluded.

Full authored text is retained for history and editorial comparison. Only supplied text and permitted anchors reach generation. Cross-deck references carry meaning-only text and no foreign visual anchors. Research sources are provenance for internally authored interpretation, never claimed quotations.

## 1. full: triad / death-temperance-star / general / rws-1909

Source ID: `tableu:triad:death-temperance-star:general:0`. Source tradition: rws-1909. Projection version: `meaning-v1`.

Projection ID: `["[\"triad\",\"death-temperance-star\",\"general\",null,\"tableu:triad:death-temperance-star:general:0\"]","meaning-v1","rws-1909"]`

Authored display/history: A clean ending makes room for repair. Let what is finished be finished (Death), then blend the lesson into your life with patience and care (Temperance). From that integration, hope returns—not as a denial of pain, but as a new, steadier orientation (The Star). Look to the skeleton on the white horse and banner, Temperance's angel pouring between cups with one foot in water and one on land, and the Star's large star over the pitchers by the pool.

Supplied title: The Healing Arc. Theme: Ending → Integration → Renewal.

Supplied text: A clean ending makes room for repair. Let what is finished be finished (Death), then blend the lesson into your life with patience and care (Temperance). From that integration, hope returns—not as a denial of pain, but as a new, steadier orientation (The Star). Look to the skeleton on the white horse and banner, Temperance's angel pouring between cups with one foot in water and one on land, and the Star's large star over the pitchers by the pool.

Supplied visual anchors: skeleton, white horse, banner, angel, cups, one foot in water, one foot on land, large star, pitchers, pool.

Runtime status: available. Meaning fidelity: pending. Absent-stage review: pending. Human semantic approval: pending.

## 4. full: triad / death-temperance-star / grief / rws-1909

Source ID: `tableu:triad:death-temperance-star:grief:1`. Source tradition: rws-1909. Projection version: `meaning-v1`.

Projection ID: `["[\"triad\",\"death-temperance-star\",\"grief\",null,\"tableu:triad:death-temperance-star:grief:1\"]","meaning-v1","rws-1909"]`

Authored display/history: This is literal healing from loss: Death marks what cannot return, Temperance asks you to metabolize grief in steady doses, and The Star brings relief and a gentler horizon. Healing is not forgetting—it is learning to live with love and memory without being consumed by the wound.

Supplied title: The Healing Arc. Theme: Ending → Integration → Renewal.

## 13. full: triad / devil-tower-sun / general / rws-1909

Source ID: `tableu:triad:devil-tower-sun:general:0`. Source tradition: rws-1909. Projection version: `meaning-v1`.

Projection ID: `["[\"triad\",\"devil-tower-sun\",\"general\",null,\"tableu:triad:devil-tower-sun:general:0\"]","meaning-v1","rws-1909"]`

Authored display/history: Notice what has you hooked (The Devil). Then tell the truth: the structure can’t hold (The Tower). The breakthrough isn’t just escape—it’s clarity and vitality returning (The Sun). If it feels like chaos, ask what honesty is being restored. The horned figure and chained figures, the lightning-split tower with falling figures, and the radiant sun over the joyful child and sunflowers mirror the release.

Supplied title: The Liberation Arc. Theme: Bondage → Rupture → Freedom.

Supplied text: Notice what has you hooked (The Devil). Then tell the truth: the structure can’t hold (The Tower). The breakthrough isn’t just escape—it’s clarity and vitality returning (The Sun). If it feels like chaos, ask what honesty is being restored. The horned figure and chained figures, the lightning-split tower with falling figures, and the radiant sun over the joyful child and sunflowers mirror the release.

Supplied visual anchors: horned figure, chained figures, lightning, tower, falling figures, radiant sun with face, joyful child, sunflowers.

## 16. full: triad / devil-tower-sun / addiction / rws-1909

Authored display/history: Withdraw to listen (The Hermit). Release the need to force an answer (The Hanged Man). Then move through ambiguity with gentleness—dreams, symbols, and intuition are part of the map (The Moon). This arc rewards patience over certainty. Anchor the inner work in the lantern and six-pointed star, the Hanged Man's halo on the tree, and the Moon's face between the two towers, the dog and wolf, and the winding path.

Supplied title: The Inner Work Arc. Theme: Solitude → Surrender → Mystery.

Supplied text: Withdraw to listen (The Hermit). Release the need to force an answer (The Hanged Man). Then move through ambiguity with gentleness—dreams, symbols, and intuition are part of the map (The Moon). This arc rewards patience over certainty. Anchor the inner work in the lantern and six-pointed star, the Hanged Man's halo on the tree, and the Moon's face between the two towers, the dog and wolf, and the winding path.

Supplied visual anchors: lantern, six-pointed star, halo, tree, full moon with face, two towers, dog and wolf, winding path.

Supplied title: The Values & Commitment Arc. Theme: Abundance → Choice → Sacred Structure.

Supplied title: The Authority & Structure Arc. Theme: Nurturing Abundance → Order & Structure → Traditional Wisdom.

## 103. full: triad / wheel-justice-hangedman / general / rws-1909

Source ID: `tableu:triad:wheel-justice-hangedman:general:0`. Source tradition: rws-1909. Projection version: `meaning-v1`.

Projection ID: `["[\"triad\",\"wheel-justice-hangedman\",\"general\",null,\"tableu:triad:wheel-justice-hangedman:general:0\"]","meaning-v1","rws-1909"]`

Authored display/history: When cycles shift beyond your control (Wheel of Fortune), face the reckoning with honesty (Justice), then release attachment to outcome (The Hanged Man). Acceptance is not defeat—it is wisdom that conserves energy for what you can influence. The turning wheel with the sphinx and snake, the scales and sword, and the Hanged Man's halo on the tree emphasize truth with surrender.

Supplied title: The Karmic Acceptance Arc. Theme: Fate Turns → Truth Demanded → Surrender to Flow.

Supplied text: When cycles shift beyond your control (Wheel of Fortune), face the reckoning with honesty (Justice), then release attachment to outcome (The Hanged Man). Acceptance is not defeat—it is wisdom that conserves energy for what you can influence. The turning wheel with the sphinx and snake, the scales and sword, and the Hanged Man's halo on the tree emphasize truth with surrender.

Supplied visual anchors: wheel, sphinx, snake, scales, sword, halo, tree.

Runtime status: available. Meaning fidelity: pending. Absent-stage review: pending. Human semantic approval: pending.

## 118. full: triad / tower-star-moon / general / rws-1909

Source ID: `tableu:triad:tower-star-moon:general:0`. Source tradition: rws-1909. Projection version: `meaning-v1`.

Projection ID: `["[\"triad\",\"tower-star-moon\",\"general\",null,\"tableu:triad:tower-star-moon:general:0\"]","meaning-v1","rws-1909"]`

Authored display/history: After structures collapse (The Tower), hope returns as a quiet compass (The Star), but the path forward remains unclear (The Moon). Trust intuition over certainty. Healing is not linear, and the next steps may only reveal themselves one at a time. The lightning-struck tower and falling figures, the Star's large star and pitchers by the pool, and the Moon's face between the two towers with the dog and wolf keep the path grounded.

Authored display/history: Letting go is not the end of the story. When the clearing is honest (Death), the next phase can be gentler and more inspired (The Star). Focus on recovery, replenishment, and a next true step. The skeleton on the white horse and banner, and the Star's large star and pitchers by the pool, point to renewal after clearing.

Supplied title: . Theme: Transformation clearing into hope.

Supplied text: Letting go is not the end of the story. When the clearing is honest (Death), the next phase can be gentler and more inspired (The Star). Focus on recovery, replenishment, and a next true step. The skeleton on the white horse and banner, and the Star's large star and pitchers by the pool, point to renewal after clearing.

Supplied visual anchors: skeleton, white horse, banner, large star, pitchers, pool.

Authored display/history: If something breaks, ask what truth it reveals. The Tower strips the false; the Sun restores the simple, bright facts. After the shake-up: simplify, tell the truth, and rebuild from what’s real. The lightning-struck tower with falling figures meets the radiant sun, joyful child, and sunflowers.

Supplied title: . Theme: Upheaval revealing clarity.

Supplied text: If something breaks, ask what truth it reveals. The Tower strips the false; the Sun restores the simple, bright facts. After the shake-up: simplify, tell the truth, and rebuild from what’s real. The lightning-struck tower with falling figures meets the radiant sun, joyful child, and sunflowers.

Supplied visual anchors: lightning, tower, falling figures, radiant sun with face, joyful child, sunflowers.

Runtime status: available. Meaning fidelity: pending. Absent-stage review: pending. Human semantic approval: pending.

Authored display/history: Necessary destruction brings authentic joy. When illusions shatter, the gift is daylight: clean facts, clear commitments, and honest repair. Let what cannot stand fall quickly, then rebuild with transparency and simple routines that support the life you actually want.

Authored display/history: Desire can clarify values, but it can also bind. When attachment tightens (The Devil), choice becomes a test (The Lovers). Name what is hooking you, then choose the path that aligns with your real values. The horned figure and chained figures stand beneath the Lovers' angel between the tree of knowledge and tree of life.

Supplied title: . Theme: Attachment patterns affecting choice.

Supplied text: Desire can clarify values, but it can also bind. When attachment tightens (The Devil), choice becomes a test (The Lovers). Name what is hooking you, then choose the path that aligns with your real values. The horned figure and chained figures stand beneath the Lovers' angel between the tree of knowledge and tree of life.

Supplied visual anchors: horned figure, chained figures, angel, tree of knowledge, tree of life.

Authored display/history: What once guided can also bind. The Hierophant offers wisdom, but the Devil reveals where doctrine has become a cage. Discern which inherited beliefs still serve you and which now limit your freedom. The Hierophant's crossed keys and pillars contrast the horned figure, inverted pentagram, and chained figures.

Supplied title: . Theme: Tradition becoming restriction.

Discern which inherited beliefs still serve you and which now limit your freedom. The Hierophant's crossed keys and pillars contrast the horned figure, inverted pentagram, and chained figures.

Authored display/history: What is hidden will come to light. The Moon walks through shadow and uncertainty; the Sun reveals the simple truth that was there all along. Trust the process—clarity follows honest confusion. The full moon with face, two towers, dog and wolf, and winding path yield to the radiant sun, joyful child, and sunflowers.

Supplied title: . Theme: Mystery yielding to illumination.

Supplied text: What is hidden will come to light. The Moon walks through shadow and uncertainty; the Sun reveals the simple truth that was there all along. Trust the process—clarity follows honest confusion. The full moon with face, two towers, dog and wolf, and winding path yield to the radiant sun, joyful child, and sunflowers.

Supplied visual anchors: full moon with face, two towers, dog and wolf, winding path, radiant sun with face, joyful child, sunflowers.

Authored display/history: Every ending opens a new beginning. The Fool leaps into the unknown; the World celebrates completion. Together they remind you that the journey is circular—mastery returns to wonder, and wonder leads to mastery. The cliff, dog, and white rose step into the dancer within the wreath under the four fixed signs.

Supplied title: . Theme: Beginning contains ending.

Authored display/history: After the clearing, the blending. Death clears what no longer serves; Temperance integrates what remains into a new wholeness. Give yourself time to absorb the change before rushing forward. The skeleton on the white horse and banner yields to Temperance's angel pouring between cups with one foot on land and one in water.

Supplied title: . Theme: Transformation finding integration.

Supplied text: After the clearing, the blending. Death clears what no longer serves; Temperance integrates what remains into a new wholeness. Give yourself time to absorb the change before rushing forward. The skeleton on the white horse and banner yields to Temperance's angel pouring between cups with one foot on land and one in water.

## 412. full: marseille-numerology / 3 / general / rws-1909

Source ID: `tableu:marseille-numerology:3:general:0`. Source tradition: marseille-classic. Projection version: `meaning-v1`.

Projection ID: `["[\"marseille-numerology\",\"3\",\"general\",null,\"tableu:marseille-numerology:3:general:0\"]","meaning-v1","rws-1909"]`

Authored display/history: Three is growth and momentum: ideas become movement, collaboration becomes creation. When Threes cluster, iterate and build. Share, refine, and let the work expand beyond the private sketch into a living form.

## 436. pair: triad / death-temperance-star / general / rws-1909

Source ID: `tableu:triad:death-temperance-star:general:0`. Source tradition: rws-1909. Projection version: `pair-v1`.

Projection ID: `["[\"triad\",\"death-temperance-star\",\"general\",[14,17],\"tableu:triad:death-temperance-star:general:0\"]","pair-v1","rws-1909"]`

Matched canonical IDs: 14, 17.

Authored display/history: A clean ending makes room for repair. Let what is finished be finished (Death), then blend the lesson into your life with patience and care (Temperance). From that integration, hope returns—not as a denial of pain, but as a new, steadier orientation (The Star). Look to the skeleton on the white horse and banner, Temperance's angel pouring between cups with one foot in water and one on land, and the Star's large star over the pitchers by the pool.

Supplied title: Balance and possibility. Theme: Balance and possibility.

Supplied text: Patient integration and hope can reinforce each other. Try one manageable adjustment that supports balance, and give a promising possibility room to develop without demanding certainty.

Supplied visual anchors: (none).

Runtime status: available. Meaning fidelity: pending. Absent-stage review: pending. Human semantic approval: pending.

Matched canonical IDs: 11, 12.

Authored display/history: When cycles shift beyond your control (Wheel of Fortune), face the reckoning with honesty (Justice), then release attachment to outcome (The Hanged Man). Acceptance is not defeat—it is wisdom that conserves energy for what you can influence. The turning wheel with the sphinx and snake, the scales and sword, and the Hanged Man's halo on the tree emphasize truth with surrender.

Supplied title: Fairness and perspective. Theme: Fairness and perspective.

Supplied text: Fairness and perspective invite a measured response. Examine the evidence, consider another angle, and allow time for a careful decision without treating delay as proof of a particular outcome.

Supplied visual anchors: (none).

Authored display/history: After structures collapse (The Tower), hope returns as a quiet compass (The Star), but the path forward remains unclear (The Moon). Trust intuition over certainty. Healing is not linear, and the next steps may only reveal themselves one at a time. The lightning-struck tower and falling figures, the Star's large star and pitchers by the pool, and the Moon's face between the two towers with the dog and wolf keep the path grounded.

Supplied title: Hope and uncertainty. Theme: Hope and uncertainty.

Supplied text: Hope and uncertainty can share the same space. Let a promising possibility guide attention while leaving room for unanswered questions, and check what you sense against what you can observe.
